        foreach (self::OPENAPI_WRITE_ONLY as $writeOnly) {
            unset($payload[$writeOnly]);
        }

        return array_map(self::openApiWireValue(...), $payload);
    }

    /**
     * One value in wire form. Recurses, because a nested DTO and a list of them are values
     * too — without it `getData()` handed back objects where every other mode gives arrays.
     */
    private static function openApiWireValue(mixed $value, ?string $temporalFormat = null): mixed
    {
        if ($value instanceof BackedEnum) {
            return $value->value;
        }
        if ($value instanceof DateTimeInterface) {
            // The schema decides the shape: `format: date` is a DATE, and writing a whole
            // timestamp for it diverged from every other mode. Sub-second precision is kept
            // only when the value carries it, so a plain timestamp is not widened with a
            // `.000000` the client never sent.
            if ($temporalFormat === 'date') {
                return $value->format('Y-m-d');
            }
            if ($temporalFormat === 'time') {
                return $value->format('H:i:sP');
            }

            return $value->format('u') === '000000'
                ? $value->format('c')
                : $value->format('Y-m-d\TH:i:s.uP');
        }
        if (is_object($value) && method_exists($value, 'getData')) {
            // A `type: object` with nothing to write is `{}`, and PHP spells that the same
            // as an empty LIST — so the empty case is cast here, the one place that still
            // knows the value came from an object schema.
            $data = $value->getData();
            return $data === [] ? (object)$data : $data;
        }
        if (is_array($value)) {
            return array_map(
                static fn(mixed $item): mixed => self::openApiWireValue($item, $temporalFormat),
                $value,
            );
        }

        return $value;
    }

    /**
     * Entered once per object by the class-level #[Callback]: `$value` IS this DTO, and the paths are
     * set by the interpreter rather than by a property label.
     */
    private function validateOpenApiConstraints(mixed $value, Callback $rule, ValidationContext $context): Result
    {
        $result = new Result();
        foreach ($this->validateOpenApiNode($this->toOpenApiValidationPayload(), self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS, 'payload', 0) as $error) {
            $result->addError(str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
        }

        return $result;
    }

    /**
     * This DTO as an OpenAPI-named payload: what it received, under the names the schema uses.
     * Machinery for the generated validation, not an API to call.
     *
     * @internal
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toOpenApiValidationPayload(): array
    {
        $payload = [];
        if ($this->hasProperty('scores')) {
            $payload['scores'] = $this->scores;
        }
        if ($this->hasProperty('scoresByKey')) {
            $payload['scoresByKey'] = $this->scoresByKey;
        }
        if ($this->hasProperty('codes')) {
            $payload['codes'] = $this->codes;
        }

        return $payload;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            return [];
        }

        $errors = [];
        $normalizedValue = $value;
        $structuredValue = $this->normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (array_key_exists('type', $schema)) {
            $typeConstraint = $schema['type'];
            if (is_string($typeConstraint)) {
                if (!$this->matchesOpenApiCallbackType($normalizedValue, $typeConstraint)) {
                    $errors[] = $typeConstraint === 'array' && is_array($normalizedValue)
                        ? sprintf('%s must be a JSON array (list with sequential keys), got an associative array', $path)
                        : sprintf('%s must be of type %s', $path, $typeConstraint);
                }
            }
        }

        if ((is_int($normalizedValue) || is_float($normalizedValue)) && $this->schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey($schema, ['minimum'])) {
            $minimum = $this->toFloatConstraint($schema['minimum'] ?? null);
            if ($minimum !== null && !($normalizedValue >= $minimum)) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s must be greater than or equal to %s', $path, $this->stringifyOpenApiNumber($minimum));
            }
        }

        if (is_string($normalizedValue) && $this->schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey($schema, ['minLength', 'pattern'])) {
            $length = mb_strlen($normalizedValue);
            $minLength = $this->toIntConstraint($schema['minLength'] ?? null);
            if ($minLength !== null && $length < $minLength) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s length must be at least %d characters', $path, $minLength);
            }
            if (is_string($pattern = $schema['pattern'] ?? null) && $pattern !== '') {
                $regex = '#' . str_replace('#', '\#', $pattern) . '#u';
                set_error_handler(static fn(): bool => true);
                try {
                    $match = preg_match($regex, $normalizedValue);
                } finally {
                    restore_error_handler();
                }

                if ($match === false) {
                    if (preg_last_error() === PREG_BAD_UTF8_ERROR) {
                        $errors[] = sprintf('%s contains invalid UTF-8 characters', $path);
                    } else {
                        $errors[] = sprintf('%s has invalid regex pattern in schema: %s', $path, $pattern);
                    }
                } elseif ($match !== 1) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s must match pattern %s', $path, $pattern);
                }
            }
        }

        if (is_array($structuredValue) && is_array($schema['required'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($schema['required'] as $requiredProp) {
                if (is_string($requiredProp) && !array_key_exists($requiredProp, $structuredValue)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s is required', $this->openApiChildPath($path, $requiredProp));
                }
            }
        }

        $definedProperties = [];
        $patternMatchedProperties = [];

        if (is_array($schema['properties'] ?? null) && is_array($structuredValue)) {
            foreach ($schema['properties'] as $propertyName => $propertySchema) {
                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($propertySchema)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $definedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                if (!array_key_exists($propertyName, $structuredValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($structuredValue[$propertyName])) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($structuredValue[$propertyName], $propertySchema, $this->openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        $listValue = is_array($value) && array_is_list($value);

        if ($listValue && is_array($schema['items'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($value as $index => $itemValue) {
                if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($itemValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($itemValue, $schema['items'], $path . '[' . $index . ']', $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        if (is_array($structuredValue)) {
            $extraPropertySchema = $schema['additionalProperties'] ?? null;
            $unevaluatedProperties = $schema['unevaluatedProperties'] ?? null;
            $evaluatedProperties = [...$definedProperties, ...$patternMatchedProperties];

            if ($extraPropertySchema === false || is_array($extraPropertySchema)) {
                foreach ($structuredValue as $propertyName => $propertyValue) {
                    if (!is_string($propertyName) || array_key_exists($propertyName, $evaluatedProperties)) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if ($extraPropertySchema === false) {
                        $errors[] = sprintf('%s has additional property "%s" which is not allowed', $path, $propertyName);
                        continue;
                    }
                    if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($propertyValue)) {
                        $evaluatedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                        continue;
                    }
                    $errors = [
                        ...$errors,
                        ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($propertyValue, $extraPropertySchema, $this->openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                    ];
                    $evaluatedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                }
            }

        }

        return $errors;
    }

    private function matchesOpenApiCallbackType(mixed $value, mixed $type): bool
    {
        if (!is_string($type) || $type === '') {
            return true;
        }

        return match ($type) {
            'integer' => is_int($value) || (is_float($value) && is_finite($value) && floor($value) === $value),
            'string' => is_string($value),
            'array' => is_array($value) && array_is_list($value),
            'object' => is_array($value) || is_object($value),
            default => true,
        };
    }

    private function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Generated DTOs expose their OpenAPI-named payload themselves.
        if (method_exists($value, 'toOpenApiValidationPayload')) {
            return $value->toOpenApiValidationPayload();
        }
        // Any other object: only its public state is visible, under PHP property names.
        return array_filter(
            get_object_vars($value),
            static fn(mixed $propertyValue): bool => $propertyValue !== null,
        );
    }

    private function isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject(mixed $value): bool
    {
        // Such a DTO validates its own schema through its own #[Assert\Callback], reached by the
        // #[Assert\Valid] cascade — the enclosing DTO must not report the same errors again.
        return is_object($value)
            && method_exists($value, 'validateOpenApiConstraints')
            && method_exists($value, 'toOpenApiValidationPayload');
    }

    private function openApiChildPath(string $path, string $propertyName): string
    {
        // The first level names the field the way the runtime validator's caller does, so both
        // modes report the same subject: `field "tags" must ...`.
        return $path === 'payload'
            ? sprintf('field "%s"', $propertyName)
            : $path . '.' . $propertyName;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<int, string> $keys
     */
    private function schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey(array $schema, array $keys): bool
    {
        foreach ($keys as $key) {
            if (array_key_exists($key, $schema)) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private function toIntConstraint(mixed $value): ?int
    {
        if (is_int($value)) {
            return $value;
        }
        if (is_float($value) && floor($value) === $value) {
            return (int)$value;
        }

        return null;
    }

    private function toFloatConstraint(mixed $value): ?float
    {
        return is_int($value) || is_float($value) ? (float)$value : null;
    }

    private function stringifyOpenApiNumber(float $value): string
    {
        $rendered = json_encode($value);
        return is_string($rendered) ? $rendered : (string)$value;
    }
}

===== NestedContainers.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use BackedEnum;
use DateTimeImmutable;
use DateTimeInterface;
use ReflectionProperty;
use Yiisoft\Input\Http\AbstractInput;
use Yiisoft\Validator\DataSetInterface;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Helper\ObjectParser;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Result;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Rule\Callback;
use Yiisoft\Validator\RulesProviderInterface;
use Yiisoft\Validator\ValidationContext;

use function array_filter;
use function array_is_list;
use function array_key_exists;
use function array_map;
use function floor;
use function get_object_vars;
use function implode;
use function in_array;
use function is_array;
use function is_finite;
use function is_float;
use function is_int;
use function is_object;
use function is_string;
use function json_encode;
use function method_exists;
use function preg_match;
use function property_exists;
use function sprintf;
use function str_ends_with;
use function var_export;

#[Callback(method: 'validateOpenApiConstraints')]
final class NestedContainers extends AbstractInput implements DataSetInterface, RulesProviderInterface
{
    /** OpenAPI name => PHP property name. */
    private const array OPENAPI_PROPERTY_NAMES = [
        'matrix' => 'matrix',
        'rowsOfMaps' => 'rowsOfMaps',
        'listsByKey' => 'listsByKey',
        'mapsByKey' => 'mapsByKey',
        'dateRows' => 'dateRows',
        'numRows' => 'numRows',
        'enumRows' => 'enumRows',
        'tagRows' => 'tagRows',
    ];

    /** Names not written out: writeOnly by definition, readOnly because a client may not set them. */
    private const array OPENAPI_WRITE_ONLY = [
    ];

    private const array OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS = [
        'type' => 'object',
        'required' => [
            'matrix',
            'rowsOfMaps',
        ],
        'properties' => [
            'matrix' => [
                'type' => 'array',
                'items' => [
                    'type' => 'array',
                    'items' => [
                        'type' => 'integer',
                        'minimum' => 0,
                    ],
                ],
            ],
            'rowsOfMaps' => [
                'type' => 'array',
                'items' => [
                    'type' => 'object',
                    'additionalProperties' => [
                        'type' => 'string',
                    ],
                ],
            ],
            'listsByKey' => [
                'type' => 'object',
                'additionalProperties' => [
                    'type' => 'array',
                    'items' => [
                        'type' => 'string',
                    ],
                ],
            ],
            'mapsByKey' => [
                'type' => 'object',
                'additionalProperties' => [
                    'type' => 'object',
                    'additionalProperties' => [
                        'type' => 'integer',
                    ],
                ],
            ],
            'dateRows' => [
                'type' => 'array',
                'items' => [
                    'type' => 'array',
                    'items' => [
                        'type' => 'string',
                        'format' => 'date',
                    ],
                ],
            ],
            'numRows' => [
                'type' => 'array',
                'items' => [
                    'type' => 'array',
                    'items' => [
                        'type' => 'number',
                    ],
                ],
            ],
            'enumRows' => [
                'type' => 'array',
                'items' => [
                    'type' => 'array',
                    'items' => [
                        'type' => 'string',
                        'enum' => [
                            'catType',
                            'dogType',
                        ],
                    ],
                ],
            ],
            'tagRows' => [
                'type' => 'array',
                'items' => [
                    'type' => 'array',
                    'items' => [
                        'type' => 'object',
                        'required' => [
                            'id',
                            'name',
                        ],
                        'properties' => [
                            'id' => [
                                'type' => 'integer',
                            ],
                            'name' => [
                                'type' => 'string',
                            ],
                        ],
                    ],
                ],
            ],
        ],
    ];

    private const int OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH = 256;

    /** @var array<array<int>> */
    public readonly array $matrix;

    /** @var array<array<string, string>> */
    public readonly array $rowsOfMaps;

    /** @var array<string, array<string>> */
    public readonly ?array $listsByKey;

    /** @var array<string, array<string, int>> */
    public readonly ?array $mapsByKey;

    /** @var array<array<string>> */
    public readonly ?array $dateRows;

    /** @var array<array<float|int>> */
    public readonly ?array $numRows;

    /** @var array<array<string>> */
    public readonly ?array $enumRows;

    /** @var array<array<Tag>> */
    public readonly ?array $tagRows;

    /** @return array<array<int>> */
    public function getMatrix(): array
    {
        return $this->matrix;
    }

    /** @return array<array<string, string>> */
    public function getRowsOfMaps(): array
    {
        return $this->rowsOfMaps;
    }

    /** @return array<string, array<string>> */
    public function getListsByKey(): ?array
    {
        return $this->hasProperty('listsByKey') ? $this->listsByKey : null;
    }

    /** Whether the payload carried `listsByKey` at all. */
    public function isListsByKeyProvided(): bool
    {
        return $this->hasProperty('listsByKey');
    }

    /** @return array<string, array<string, int>> */
    public function getMapsByKey(): ?array
    {
        return $this->hasProperty('mapsByKey') ? $this->mapsByKey : null;
    }

    /** Whether the payload carried `mapsByKey` at all. */
    public function isMapsByKeyProvided(): bool
    {
        return $this->hasProperty('mapsByKey');
    }

    /** @return array<array<string>> */
    public function getDateRows(): ?array
    {
        return $this->hasProperty('dateRows') ? $this->dateRows : null;
    }

    /** Whether the payload carried `dateRows` at all. */
    public function isDateRowsProvided(): bool
    {
        return $this->hasProperty('dateRows');
    }

    /** @return array<array<float|int>> */
    public function getNumRows(): ?array
    {
        return $this->hasProperty('numRows') ? $this->numRows : null;
    }

    /** Whether the payload carried `numRows` at all. */
    public function isNumRowsProvided(): bool
    {
        return $this->hasProperty('numRows');
    }

    /** @return array<array<string>> */
    public function getEnumRows(): ?array
    {
        return $this->hasProperty('enumRows') ? $this->enumRows : null;
    }

    /** Whether the payload carried `enumRows` at all. */
    public function isEnumRowsProvided(): bool
    {
        return $this->hasProperty('enumRows');
    }

    /** @return array<array<Tag>> */
    public function getTagRows(): ?array
    {
        return $this->hasProperty('tagRows') ? $this->tagRows : null;
    }

    /** Whether the payload carried `tagRows` at all. */
    public function isTagRowsProvided(): bool
    {
        return $this->hasProperty('tagRows');
    }

    /**
     * Rules come from the attributes above; this only re-exposes them. A class that provides a
     * data set has its own attribute parsing skipped by the validator, so without this method
     * NOTHING would be validated — not even a missing required property.
     */
    public function getRules(): iterable
    {
        return (new ObjectParser($this))->getRules();
    }

    /**
     * Whether the payload carried this property at all.
     *
     * An unhydrated typed property is UNINITIALISED, which is the language's own record of
     * what the payload did not contain. A property sent as null IS initialised, so the two
     * are distinguishable without any sentinel value.
     */
    public function hasProperty(string $property): bool
    {
        $name = self::OPENAPI_PROPERTY_NAMES[$property] ?? $property;

        return property_exists($this, $name)
            && (new ReflectionProperty($this, $name))->isInitialized($this);
    }

    public function getPropertyValue(string $property): mixed
    {
        return $this->hasProperty($property)
            ? $this->{self::OPENAPI_PROPERTY_NAMES[$property] ?? $property}
            : null;
    }

    /**
     * What the object holds, under the names the schema uses, in WIRE form.
     *
     * Yii3 ships no response normalizer, so this doubles as the array form: a backed enum
     * becomes its value and a date its ISO-8601 string, which is what every other mode
     * produces. The interpreter reads `toOpenApiValidationPayload()` instead and sees the
     * values as held.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function getData(): ?array
    {
        $payload = $this->toOpenApiValidationPayload();
        foreach (self::OPENAPI_WRITE_ONLY as $writeOnly) {
            unset($payload[$writeOnly]);
        }

        return array_map(self::openApiWireValue(...), $payload);
    }

    /**
     * One value in wire form. Recurses, because a nested DTO and a list of them are values
     * too — without it `getData()` handed back objects where every other mode gives arrays.
     */
    private static function openApiWireValue(mixed $value, ?string $temporalFormat = null): mixed
    {
        if ($value instanceof BackedEnum) {
            return $value->value;
        }
        if ($value instanceof DateTimeInterface) {
            // The schema decides the shape: `format: date` is a DATE, and writing a whole
            // timestamp for it diverged from every other mode. Sub-second precision is kept
            // only when the value carries it, so a plain timestamp is not widened with a
            // `.000000` the client never sent.
            if ($temporalFormat === 'date') {
                return $value->format('Y-m-d');
            }
            if ($temporalFormat === 'time') {
                return $value->format('H:i:sP');
            }

            return $value->format('u') === '000000'
                ? $value->format('c')
                : $value->format('Y-m-d\TH:i:s.uP');
        }
        if (is_object($value) && method_exists($value, 'getData')) {
            // A `type: object` with nothing to write is `{}`, and PHP spells that the same
            // as an empty LIST — so the empty case is cast here, the one place that still
            // knows the value came from an object schema.
            $data = $value->getData();
            return $data === [] ? (object)$data : $data;
        }
        if (is_array($value)) {
            return array_map(
                static fn(mixed $item): mixed => self::openApiWireValue($item, $temporalFormat),
                $value,
            );
        }

        return $value;
    }

    /**
     * Entered once per object by the class-level #[Callback]: `$value` IS this DTO, and the paths are
     * set by the interpreter rather than by a property label.
     */
    private function validateOpenApiConstraints(mixed $value, Callback $rule, ValidationContext $context): Result
    {
        $result = new Result();
        foreach ($this->validateOpenApiNode($this->toOpenApiValidationPayload(), self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS, 'payload', 0) as $error) {
            $result->addError(str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
        }

        return $result;
    }

    /**
     * This DTO as an OpenAPI-named payload: what it received, under the names the schema uses.
     * Machinery for the generated validation, not an API to call.
     *
     * @internal
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toOpenApiValidationPayload(): array
    {
        $payload = [];
        if ($this->hasProperty('matrix')) {
            $payload['matrix'] = $this->matrix;
        }
        if ($this->hasProperty('rowsOfMaps')) {
            $payload['rowsOfMaps'] = $this->rowsOfMaps;
        }
        if ($this->hasProperty('listsByKey')) {
            $payload['listsByKey'] = $this->listsByKey;
        }
        if ($this->hasProperty('mapsByKey')) {
            $payload['mapsByKey'] = $this->mapsByKey;
        }
        if ($this->hasProperty('dateRows')) {
            $payload['dateRows'] = $this->dateRows;
        }
        if ($this->hasProperty('numRows')) {
            $payload['numRows'] = $this->numRows;
        }
        if ($this->hasProperty('enumRows')) {
            $payload['enumRows'] = $this->enumRows;
        }
        if ($this->hasProperty('tagRows')) {
            $payload['tagRows'] = $this->tagRows;
        }

        return $payload;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            return [];
        }

        $errors = [];
        $normalizedValue = $value;
        $structuredValue = $this->normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (is_array($schema['enum'] ?? null) && !in_array($normalizedValue, $schema['enum'], true)) {
            $allowed = implode(', ', array_map(
                static function (mixed $allowedValue): string {
                    $json = json_encode($allowedValue);
                    return $json !== false ? $json : var_export($allowedValue, true);
                },
                $schema['enum'],
            ));
            $errors[] = sprintf('%s must be one of: %s', $path, $allowed);
        }

        if (array_key_exists('type', $schema)) {
            $typeConstraint = $schema['type'];
            if (is_string($typeConstraint)) {
                if (!$this->matchesOpenApiCallbackType($normalizedValue, $typeConstraint)) {
                    $errors[] = $typeConstraint === 'array' && is_array($normalizedValue)
                        ? sprintf('%s must be a JSON array (list with sequential keys), got an associative array', $path)
                        : sprintf('%s must be of type %s', $path, $typeConstraint);
                }
            }
        }

        if ((is_int($normalizedValue) || is_float($normalizedValue)) && $this->schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey($schema, ['minimum'])) {
            $minimum = $this->toFloatConstraint($schema['minimum'] ?? null);
            if ($minimum !== null && !($normalizedValue >= $minimum)) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s must be greater than or equal to %s', $path, $this->stringifyOpenApiNumber($minimum));
            }
        }

        if (is_string($normalizedValue) && $this->schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey($schema, ['format'])) {
            if (is_string($format = $schema['format'] ?? null) && !$this->isValidOpenApiStringFormat($normalizedValue, $format)) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s must match format %s', $path, $format);
            }
        }

        if (is_array($structuredValue) && is_array($schema['required'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($schema['required'] as $requiredProp) {
                if (is_string($requiredProp) && !array_key_exists($requiredProp, $structuredValue)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s is required', $this->openApiChildPath($path, $requiredProp));
                }
            }
        }

        $definedProperties = [];
        $patternMatchedProperties = [];

        if (is_array($schema['properties'] ?? null) && is_array($structuredValue)) {
            foreach ($schema['properties'] as $propertyName => $propertySchema) {
                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($propertySchema)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $definedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                if (!array_key_exists($propertyName, $structuredValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($structuredValue[$propertyName])) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($structuredValue[$propertyName], $propertySchema, $this->openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        $listValue = is_array($value) && array_is_list($value);

        if ($listValue && is_array($schema['items'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($value as $index => $itemValue) {
                if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($itemValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($itemValue, $schema['items'], $path . '[' . $index . ']', $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        if (is_array($structuredValue)) {
            $extraPropertySchema = $schema['additionalProperties'] ?? null;
            $unevaluatedProperties = $schema['unevaluatedProperties'] ?? null;
            $evaluatedProperties = [...$definedProperties, ...$patternMatchedProperties];

            if ($extraPropertySchema === false || is_array($extraPropertySchema)) {
                foreach ($structuredValue as $propertyName => $propertyValue) {
                    if (!is_string($propertyName) || array_key_exists($propertyName, $evaluatedProperties)) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if ($extraPropertySchema === false) {
                        $errors[] = sprintf('%s has additional property "%s" which is not allowed', $path, $propertyName);
                        continue;
                    }
                    if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($propertyValue)) {
                        $evaluatedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                        continue;
                    }
                    $errors = [
                        ...$errors,
                        ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($propertyValue, $extraPropertySchema, $this->openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                    ];
                    $evaluatedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                }
            }

        }

        return $errors;
    }

    private function matchesOpenApiCallbackType(mixed $value, mixed $type): bool
    {
        if (!is_string($type) || $type === '') {
            return true;
        }

        return match ($type) {
            'integer' => is_int($value) || (is_float($value) && is_finite($value) && floor($value) === $value),
            'number' => is_int($value) || is_float($value),
            'string' => is_string($value),
            'array' => is_array($value) && array_is_list($value),
            'object' => is_array($value) || is_object($value),
            default => true,
        };
    }

    private function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Generated DTOs expose their OpenAPI-named payload themselves.
        if (method_exists($value, 'toOpenApiValidationPayload')) {
            return $value->toOpenApiValidationPayload();
        }
        // Any other object: only its public state is visible, under PHP property names.
        return array_filter(
            get_object_vars($value),
            static fn(mixed $propertyValue): bool => $propertyValue !== null,
        );
    }

    private function isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject(mixed $value): bool
    {
        // Such a DTO validates its own schema through its own #[Assert\Callback], reached by the
        // #[Assert\Valid] cascade — the enclosing DTO must not report the same errors again.
        return is_object($value)
            && method_exists($value, 'validateOpenApiConstraints')
            && method_exists($value, 'toOpenApiValidationPayload');
    }

    private function openApiChildPath(string $path, string $propertyName): string
    {
        // The first level names the field the way the runtime validator's caller does, so both
        // modes report the same subject: `field "tags" must ...`.
        return $path === 'payload'
            ? sprintf('field "%s"', $propertyName)
            : $path . '.' . $propertyName;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<int, string> $keys
     */
    private function schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey(array $schema, array $keys): bool
    {
        foreach ($keys as $key) {
            if (array_key_exists($key, $schema)) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private function toFloatConstraint(mixed $value): ?float
    {
        return is_int($value) || is_float($value) ? (float)$value : null;
    }

    private function stringifyOpenApiNumber(float $value): string
    {
        $rendered = json_encode($value);
        return is_string($rendered) ? $rendered : (string)$value;
    }

    private function isValidOpenApiStringFormat(string $value, string $format): bool
    {
        return match ($format) {
            'date' => $this->isValidOpenApiDateFormat($value),
            default => true,
        };
    }

    private function isValidOpenApiDateFormat(string $value): bool
    {
        if (!preg_match('/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/', $value)) {
            return false;
        }
        $date = DateTimeImmutable::createFromFormat('Y-m-d', $value);
        return $date !== false && $date->format('Y-m-d') === $value;
    }
}

===== NullableContainerValues.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use BackedEnum;
use DateTimeInterface;
use ReflectionProperty;
use Yiisoft\Input\Http\AbstractInput;
use Yiisoft\Validator\DataSetInterface;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Helper\ObjectParser;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Result;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Rule\Callback;
use Yiisoft\Validator\RulesProviderInterface;
use Yiisoft\Validator\ValidationContext;

use function array_filter;
use function array_is_list;
use function array_key_exists;
use function array_map;
use function floor;
use function get_object_vars;
use function is_array;
use function is_finite;
use function is_float;
use function is_int;
use function is_object;
use function is_string;
use function json_encode;
use function mb_strlen;
use function method_exists;
use function property_exists;
use function sprintf;
use function str_ends_with;

#[Callback(method: 'validateOpenApiConstraints')]
final class NullableContainerValues extends AbstractInput implements DataSetInterface, RulesProviderInterface
{
    /** OpenAPI name => PHP property name. */
    private const array OPENAPI_PROPERTY_NAMES = [
        'listOfNullable' => 'listOfNullable',
        'mapOfNullable' => 'mapOfNullable',
        'listOfNullable31' => 'listOfNullable31',
        'mapOfNullable31' => 'mapOfNullable31',
    ];

    /** Names not written out: writeOnly by definition, readOnly because a client may not set them. */
    private const array OPENAPI_WRITE_ONLY = [
    ];

    private const array OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS = [
        'type' => 'object',
        'required' => [
            'listOfNullable',
        ],
        'properties' => [
            'listOfNullable' => [
                'type' => 'array',
                'items' => [
                    'type' => 'string',
                    'nullable' => true,
                    'minLength' => 2,
                ],
            ],
            'mapOfNullable' => [
                'type' => 'object',
                'additionalProperties' => [
                    'type' => 'integer',
                    'nullable' => true,
                    'minimum' => 5,
                ],
            ],
            'listOfNullable31' => [
                'type' => 'array',
                'items' => [
                    'type' => 'string',
                    'nullable' => true,
                ],
            ],
            'mapOfNullable31' => [
                'type' => 'object',
                'additionalProperties' => [
                    'type' => 'integer',
                    'nullable' => true,
                ],
            ],
        ],
    ];

    private const int OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH = 256;

    /** @var array<?string> */
    public readonly array $listOfNullable;

    /** @var array<string, ?int> */
    public readonly ?array $mapOfNullable;

    /** @var array<?string> */
    public readonly ?array $listOfNullable31;

    /** @var array<string, ?int> */
    public readonly ?array $mapOfNullable31;

    /** @return array<?string> */
    public function getListOfNullable(): array
    {
        return $this->listOfNullable;
    }

    /** @return array<string, ?int> */
    public function getMapOfNullable(): ?array
    {
        return $this->hasProperty('mapOfNullable') ? $this->mapOfNullable : null;
    }

    /** Whether the payload carried `mapOfNullable` at all. */
    public function isMapOfNullableProvided(): bool
    {
        return $this->hasProperty('mapOfNullable');
    }

    /** @return array<?string> */
    public function getListOfNullable31(): ?array
    {
        return $this->hasProperty('listOfNullable31') ? $this->listOfNullable31 : null;
    }

    /** Whether the payload carried `listOfNullable31` at all. */
    public function isListOfNullable31Provided(): bool
    {
        return $this->hasProperty('listOfNullable31');
    }

    /** @return array<string, ?int> */
    public function getMapOfNullable31(): ?array
    {
        return $this->hasProperty('mapOfNullable31') ? $this->mapOfNullable31 : null;
    }

    /** Whether the payload carried `mapOfNullable31` at all. */
    public function isMapOfNullable31Provided(): bool
    {
        return $this->hasProperty('mapOfNullable31');
    }

    /**
     * Rules come from the attributes above; this only re-exposes them. A class that provides a
     * data set has its own attribute parsing skipped by the validator, so without this method
     * NOTHING would be validated — not even a missing required property.
     */
    public function getRules(): iterable
    {
        return (new ObjectParser($this))->getRules();
    }

    /**
     * Whether the payload carried this property at all.
     *
     * An unhydrated typed property is UNINITIALISED, which is the language's own record of
     * what the payload did not contain. A property sent as null IS initialised, so the two
     * are distinguishable without any sentinel value.
     */
    public function hasProperty(string $property): bool
    {
        $name = self::OPENAPI_PROPERTY_NAMES[$property] ?? $property;

        return property_exists($this, $name)
            && (new ReflectionProperty($this, $name))->isInitialized($this);
    }

    public function getPropertyValue(string $property): mixed
    {
        return $this->hasProperty($property)
            ? $this->{self::OPENAPI_PROPERTY_NAMES[$property] ?? $property}
            : null;
    }

    /**
     * What the object holds, under the names the schema uses, in WIRE form.
     *
     * Yii3 ships no response normalizer, so this doubles as the array form: a backed enum
     * becomes its value and a date its ISO-8601 string, which is what every other mode
     * produces. The interpreter reads `toOpenApiValidationPayload()` instead and sees the
     * values as held.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function getData(): ?array
    {
        $payload = $this->toOpenApiValidationPayload();
        foreach (self::OPENAPI_WRITE_ONLY as $writeOnly) {
            unset($payload[$writeOnly]);
        }

        return array_map(self::openApiWireValue(...), $payload);
    }

    /**
     * One value in wire form. Recurses, because a nested DTO and a list of them are values
     * too — without it `getData()` handed back objects where every other mode gives arrays.
     */
    private static function openApiWireValue(mixed $value, ?string $temporalFormat = null): mixed
    {
        if ($value instanceof BackedEnum) {
            return $value->value;
        }
        if ($value instanceof DateTimeInterface) {
            // The schema decides the shape: `format: date` is a DATE, and writing a whole
            // timestamp for it diverged from every other mode. Sub-second precision is kept
            // only when the value carries it, so a plain timestamp is not widened with a
            // `.000000` the client never sent.
            if ($temporalFormat === 'date') {
                return $value->format('Y-m-d');
            }
            if ($temporalFormat === 'time') {
                return $value->format('H:i:sP');
            }

            return $value->format('u') === '000000'
                ? $value->format('c')
                : $value->format('Y-m-d\TH:i:s.uP');
        }
        if (is_object($value) && method_exists($value, 'getData')) {
            // A `type: object` with nothing to write is `{}`, and PHP spells that the same
            // as an empty LIST — so the empty case is cast here, the one place that still
            // knows the value came from an object schema.
            $data = $value->getData();
            return $data === [] ? (object)$data : $data;
        }
        if (is_array($value)) {
            return array_map(
                static fn(mixed $item): mixed => self::openApiWireValue($item, $temporalFormat),
                $value,
            );
        }

        return $value;
    }

    /**
     * Entered once per object by the class-level #[Callback]: `$value` IS this DTO, and the paths are
     * set by the interpreter rather than by a property label.
     */
    private function validateOpenApiConstraints(mixed $value, Callback $rule, ValidationContext $context): Result
    {
        $result = new Result();
        foreach ($this->validateOpenApiNode($this->toOpenApiValidationPayload(), self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS, 'payload', 0) as $error) {
            $result->addError(str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
        }

        return $result;
    }

    /**
     * This DTO as an OpenAPI-named payload: what it received, under the names the schema uses.
     * Machinery for the generated validation, not an API to call.
     *
     * @internal
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toOpenApiValidationPayload(): array
    {
        $payload = [];
        if ($this->hasProperty('listOfNullable')) {
            $payload['listOfNullable'] = $this->listOfNullable;
        }
        if ($this->hasProperty('mapOfNullable')) {
            $payload['mapOfNullable'] = $this->mapOfNullable;
        }
        if ($this->hasProperty('listOfNullable31')) {
            $payload['listOfNullable31'] = $this->listOfNullable31;
        }
        if ($this->hasProperty('mapOfNullable31')) {
            $payload['mapOfNullable31'] = $this->mapOfNullable31;
        }

        return $payload;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            return [];
        }

        $errors = [];
        if ($value === null && ($schema['nullable'] ?? false) === true) {
            return [];
        }

        $normalizedValue = $value;
        $structuredValue = $this->normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (array_key_exists('type', $schema)) {
            $typeConstraint = $schema['type'];
            if (is_string($typeConstraint)) {
                if (!$this->matchesOpenApiCallbackType($normalizedValue, $typeConstraint)) {
                    $errors[] = $typeConstraint === 'array' && is_array($normalizedValue)
                        ? sprintf('%s must be a JSON array (list with sequential keys), got an associative array', $path)
                        : sprintf('%s must be of type %s', $path, $typeConstraint);
                }
            }
        }

        if ((is_int($normalizedValue) || is_float($normalizedValue)) && $this->schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey($schema, ['minimum'])) {
            $minimum = $this->toFloatConstraint($schema['minimum'] ?? null);
            if ($minimum !== null && !($normalizedValue >= $minimum)) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s must be greater than or equal to %s', $path, $this->stringifyOpenApiNumber($minimum));
            }
        }

        if (is_string($normalizedValue) && $this->schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey($schema, ['minLength'])) {
            $length = mb_strlen($normalizedValue);
            $minLength = $this->toIntConstraint($schema['minLength'] ?? null);
            if ($minLength !== null && $length < $minLength) {
                $errors[] = sprintf('%s length must be at least %d characters', $path, $minLength);
            }
        }

        if (is_array($structuredValue) && is_array($schema['required'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($schema['required'] as $requiredProp) {
                if (is_string($requiredProp) && !array_key_exists($requiredProp, $structuredValue)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s is required', $this->openApiChildPath($path, $requiredProp));
                }
            }
        }

        $definedProperties = [];
        $patternMatchedProperties = [];

        if (is_array($schema['properties'] ?? null) && is_array($structuredValue)) {
            foreach ($schema['properties'] as $propertyName => $propertySchema) {
                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($propertySchema)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $definedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                if (!array_key_exists($propertyName, $structuredValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($structuredValue[$propertyName])) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($structuredValue[$propertyName], $propertySchema, $this->openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        $listValue = is_array($value) && array_is_list($value);

        if ($listValue && is_array($schema['items'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($value as $index => $itemValue) {
                if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($itemValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($itemValue, $schema['items'], $path . '[' . $index . ']', $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        if (is_array($structuredValue)) {
            $extraPropertySchema = $schema['additionalProperties'] ?? null;
            $unevaluatedProperties = $schema['unevaluatedProperties'] ?? null;
            $evaluatedProperties = [...$definedProperties, ...$patternMatchedProperties];

            if ($extraPropertySchema === false || is_array($extraPropertySchema)) {
                foreach ($structuredValue as $propertyName => $propertyValue) {
                    if (!is_string($propertyName) || array_key_exists($propertyName, $evaluatedProperties)) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if ($extraPropertySchema === false) {
                        $errors[] = sprintf('%s has additional property "%s" which is not allowed', $path, $propertyName);
                        continue;
                    }
                    if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($propertyValue)) {
                        $evaluatedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                        continue;
                    }
                    $errors = [
                        ...$errors,
                        ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($propertyValue, $extraPropertySchema, $this->openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                    ];
                    $evaluatedProperties[$propertyName] = true;
                }
            }

        }

        return $errors;
    }

    private function matchesOpenApiCallbackType(mixed $value, mixed $type): bool
    {
        if (!is_string($type) || $type === '') {
            return true;
        }

        return match ($type) {
            'integer' => is_int($value) || (is_float($value) && is_finite($value) && floor($value) === $value),
            'string' => is_string($value),
            'array' => is_array($value) && array_is_list($value),
            'object' => is_array($value) || is_object($value),
            default => true,
        };
    }

    private function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Generated DTOs expose their OpenAPI-named payload themselves.
        if (method_exists($value, 'toOpenApiValidationPayload')) {
            return $value->toOpenApiValidationPayload();
        }
        // Any other object: only its public state is visible, under PHP property names.
        return array_filter(
            get_object_vars($value),
            static fn(mixed $propertyValue): bool => $propertyValue !== null,
        );
    }

    private function isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject(mixed $value): bool
    {
        // Such a DTO validates its own schema through its own #[Assert\Callback], reached by the
        // #[Assert\Valid] cascade — the enclosing DTO must not report the same errors again.
        return is_object($value)
            && method_exists($value, 'validateOpenApiConstraints')
            && method_exists($value, 'toOpenApiValidationPayload');
    }

    private function openApiChildPath(string $path, string $propertyName): string
    {
        // The first level names the field the way the runtime validator's caller does, so both
        // modes report the same subject: `field "tags" must ...`.
        return $path === 'payload'
            ? sprintf('field "%s"', $propertyName)
            : $path . '.' . $propertyName;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<int, string> $keys
     */
    private function schemaHasAnyOpenApiKey(array $schema, array $keys): bool
    {
        foreach ($keys as $key) {
            if (array_key_exists($key, $schema)) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private function toIntConstraint(mixed $value): ?int
    {
        if (is_int($value)) {
            return $value;
        }
        if (is_float($value) && floor($value) === $value) {
            return (int)$value;
        }

        return null;
    }

    private function toFloatConstraint(mixed $value): ?float
    {
        return is_int($value) || is_float($value) ? (float)$value : null;
    }

    private function stringifyOpenApiNumber(float $value): string
    {
        $rendered = json_encode($value);
        return is_string($rendered) ? $rendered : (string)$value;
    }
}

===== Pet.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use BackedEnum;
use DateTimeInterface;
use ReflectionProperty;
use Yiisoft\Input\Http\AbstractInput;
use Yiisoft\Validator\DataSetInterface;
use Yiisoft\Validator\EmptyCondition\WhenNull;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Helper\ObjectParser;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Result;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Rule\Callback;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Rule\StringValue;
use Yiisoft\Validator\RulesProviderInterface;
use Yiisoft\Validator\ValidationContext;

use function array_filter;
use function array_key_exists;
use function array_map;
use function get_object_vars;
use function is_array;
use function is_object;
use function is_string;
use function method_exists;
use function property_exists;
use function sprintf;
use function str_ends_with;

#[Callback(method: 'validateOpenApiConstraints')]
final class Pet extends AbstractInput implements DataSetInterface, RulesProviderInterface
{
    /** OpenAPI name => PHP property name. */
    private const array OPENAPI_PROPERTY_NAMES = [
        'name' => 'name',
    ];

    /** Names not written out: writeOnly by definition, readOnly because a client may not set them. */
    private const array OPENAPI_WRITE_ONLY = [
    ];

    private const array OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS = [
        'type' => 'object',
        'required' => [
            'name',
        ],
        'properties' => [
            'name' => [],
        ],
    ];

    private const int OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH = 256;

    #[StringValue(skipOnEmpty: new WhenNull())]
    public readonly string $name;

    public function getName(): string
    {
        return $this->name;
    }

    /**
     * Rules come from the attributes above; this only re-exposes them. A class that provides a
     * data set has its own attribute parsing skipped by the validator, so without this method
     * NOTHING would be validated — not even a missing required property.
     */
    public function getRules(): iterable
    {
        return (new ObjectParser($this))->getRules();
    }

    /**
     * Whether the payload carried this property at all.
     *
     * An unhydrated typed property is UNINITIALISED, which is the language's own record of
     * what the payload did not contain. A property sent as null IS initialised, so the two
     * are distinguishable without any sentinel value.
     */

    {
        $name = self::OPENAPI_PROPERTY_NAMES[$property] ?? $property;

        return property_exists($this, $name)
            && (new ReflectionProperty($this, $name))->isInitialized($this);
    }

    public function getPropertyValue(string $property): mixed
    {
        return $this->hasProperty($property)
            ? $this->{self::OPENAPI_PROPERTY_NAMES[$property] ?? $property}
            : null;
    }

    /**
     * What the object holds, under the names the schema uses, in WIRE form.
     *
     * Yii3 ships no response normalizer, so this doubles as the array form: a backed enum
     * becomes its value and a date its ISO-8601 string, which is what every other mode
     * produces. The interpreter reads `toOpenApiValidationPayload()` instead and sees the
     * values as held.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function getData(): ?array
    {
        $payload = $this->toOpenApiValidationPayload();
        foreach (self::OPENAPI_WRITE_ONLY as $writeOnly) {
            unset($payload[$writeOnly]);
        }

        return array_map(self::openApiWireValue(...), $payload);
    }

    /**
     * One value in wire form. Recurses, because a nested DTO and a list of them are values
     * too — without it `getData()` handed back objects where every other mode gives arrays.
     */
    private static function openApiWireValue(mixed $value, ?string $temporalFormat = null): mixed
    {
        if ($value instanceof BackedEnum) {
            return $value->value;
        }
        if ($value instanceof DateTimeInterface) {
            // The schema decides the shape: `format: date` is a DATE, and writing a whole
            // timestamp for it diverged from every other mode. Sub-second precision is kept
            // only when the value carries it, so a plain timestamp is not widened with a
            // `.000000` the client never sent.
            if ($temporalFormat === 'date') {
                return $value->format('Y-m-d');
            }
            if ($temporalFormat === 'time') {
                return $value->format('H:i:sP');
            }

            return $value->format('u') === '000000'
                ? $value->format('c')
                : $value->format('Y-m-d\TH:i:s.uP');
        }
        if (is_object($value) && method_exists($value, 'getData')) {
            // A `type: object` with nothing to write is `{}`, and PHP spells that the same
            // as an empty LIST — so the empty case is cast here, the one place that still
            // knows the value came from an object schema.
            $data = $value->getData();
            return $data === [] ? (object)$data : $data;
        }
        if (is_array($value)) {
            return array_map(
                static fn(mixed $item): mixed => self::openApiWireValue($item, $temporalFormat),
                $value,
            );
        }

        return $value;
    }

    /**
     * Entered once per object by the class-level #[Callback]: `$value` IS this DTO, and the paths are
     * set by the interpreter rather than by a property label.
     */
    private function validateOpenApiConstraints(mixed $value, Callback $rule, ValidationContext $context): Result
    {
        $result = new Result();
        foreach ($this->validateOpenApiNode($this->toOpenApiValidationPayload(), self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS, 'payload', 0) as $error) {
            $result->addError(str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
        }

        return $result;
    }

    /**
     * This DTO as an OpenAPI-named payload: what it received, under the names the schema uses.
     * Machinery for the generated validation, not an API to call.
     *
     * @internal
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toOpenApiValidationPayload(): array
    {
        $payload = [];
        if ($this->hasProperty('name')) {
            $payload['name'] = $this->name;
        }

        return $payload;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            return [];
        }

        $errors = [];
        $normalizedValue = $value;
        $structuredValue = $this->normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (array_key_exists('type', $schema)) {
            $typeConstraint = $schema['type'];
            if (is_string($typeConstraint)) {
                if (!$this->matchesOpenApiCallbackType($normalizedValue, $typeConstraint)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s must be of type %s', $path, $typeConstraint);
                }
            }
        }

        if (is_array($structuredValue) && is_array($schema['required'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($schema['required'] as $requiredProp) {
                if (is_string($requiredProp) && !array_key_exists($requiredProp, $structuredValue)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s is required', $this->openApiChildPath($path, $requiredProp));
                }
            }
        }

        if (is_array($schema['properties'] ?? null) && is_array($structuredValue)) {
            foreach ($schema['properties'] as $propertyName => $propertySchema) {
                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($propertySchema)) {
                    continue;
                }
                if (!array_key_exists($propertyName, $structuredValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($structuredValue[$propertyName])) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($structuredValue[$propertyName], $propertySchema, $this->openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        return $errors;
    }

    private function matchesOpenApiCallbackType(mixed $value, mixed $type): bool
    {
        if (!is_string($type) || $type === '') {
            return true;
        }

        return match ($type) {
            'object' => is_array($value) || is_object($value),
            default => true,
        };
    }

    private function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Generated DTOs expose their OpenAPI-named payload themselves.
        if (method_exists($value, 'toOpenApiValidationPayload')) {
            return $value->toOpenApiValidationPayload();
        }
        // Any other object: only its public state is visible, under PHP property names.
        return array_filter(
            get_object_vars($value),
            static fn(mixed $propertyValue): bool => $propertyValue !== null,
        );
    }

    private function isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject(mixed $value): bool
    {
        // Such a DTO validates its own schema through its own #[Assert\Callback], reached by the
        // #[Assert\Valid] cascade — the enclosing DTO must not report the same errors again.
        return is_object($value)
            && method_exists($value, 'validateOpenApiConstraints')
            && method_exists($value, 'toOpenApiValidationPayload');
    }

    private function openApiChildPath(string $path, string $propertyName): string
    {
        // The first level names the field the way the runtime validator's caller does, so both
        // modes report the same subject: `field "tags" must ...`.
        return $path === 'payload'
            ? sprintf('field "%s"', $propertyName)
            : $path . '.' . $propertyName;
    }
}

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

/**
 * NOTE: This DTO is auto-generated from an OpenAPI schema.
 * Do not edit this file manually.
 *
 * Members: Circle|Square

 * Discriminated by `kind`; the branch is chosen by the emitted interpreter, not by an attribute — Yii3 has
 * no discriminator mapping of its own.

}

===== Square.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use BackedEnum;
use DateTimeInterface;
use ReflectionProperty;
use Yiisoft\Input\Http\AbstractInput;
use Yiisoft\Validator\DataSetInterface;
use Yiisoft\Validator\EmptyCondition\WhenNull;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Helper\ObjectParser;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Result;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Rule\Callback;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Rule\Integer;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Rule\StringValue;
use Yiisoft\Validator\RulesProviderInterface;
use Yiisoft\Validator\ValidationContext;

use function array_filter;
use function array_key_exists;
use function array_map;
use function get_object_vars;
use function is_array;
use function is_object;
use function is_string;
use function method_exists;
use function property_exists;
use function sprintf;
use function str_ends_with;

#[Callback(method: 'validateOpenApiConstraints')]
final class Square extends AbstractInput implements Shape, DataSetInterface, RulesProviderInterface
{
    /** OpenAPI name => PHP property name. */
    private const array OPENAPI_PROPERTY_NAMES = [
        'kind' => 'kind',
        'side' => 'side',
    ];

    /** Names not written out: writeOnly by definition, readOnly because a client may not set them. */
    private const array OPENAPI_WRITE_ONLY = [
    ];

    private const array OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS = [
        'type' => 'object',
        'required' => [
            'kind',
            'side',
        ],
        'properties' => [
            'kind' => [],
            'side' => [],
        ],
    ];

    private const int OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH = 256;

    #[StringValue(skipOnEmpty: new WhenNull())]
    public readonly string $kind;

    #[Integer(skipOnEmpty: new WhenNull())]
    public readonly int $side;

    public function getKind(): string
    {
        return $this->kind;
    }

    public function getSide(): int
    {
        return $this->side;
    }

    /**
     * Rules come from the attributes above; this only re-exposes them. A class that provides a
     * data set has its own attribute parsing skipped by the validator, so without this method
     * NOTHING would be validated — not even a missing required property.
     */
    public function getRules(): iterable
    {
        return (new ObjectParser($this))->getRules();
    }

    /**
     * Whether the payload carried this property at all.
     *
     * An unhydrated typed property is UNINITIALISED, which is the language's own record of
     * what the payload did not contain. A property sent as null IS initialised, so the two
     * are distinguishable without any sentinel value.
     */
    public function hasProperty(string $property): bool
    {
        $name = self::OPENAPI_PROPERTY_NAMES[$property] ?? $property;

        return property_exists($this, $name)
            && (new ReflectionProperty($this, $name))->isInitialized($this);
    }

    public function getPropertyValue(string $property): mixed
    {
        return $this->hasProperty($property)
            ? $this->{self::OPENAPI_PROPERTY_NAMES[$property] ?? $property}
            : null;
    }

    /**
     * What the object holds, under the names the schema uses, in WIRE form.
     *
     * Yii3 ships no response normalizer, so this doubles as the array form: a backed enum
     * becomes its value and a date its ISO-8601 string, which is what every other mode
     * produces. The interpreter reads `toOpenApiValidationPayload()` instead and sees the
     * values as held.
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function getData(): ?array
    {
        $payload = $this->toOpenApiValidationPayload();
        foreach (self::OPENAPI_WRITE_ONLY as $writeOnly) {
            unset($payload[$writeOnly]);
        }

        return array_map(self::openApiWireValue(...), $payload);
    }

    /**
     * One value in wire form. Recurses, because a nested DTO and a list of them are values
     * too — without it `getData()` handed back objects where every other mode gives arrays.
     */
    private static function openApiWireValue(mixed $value, ?string $temporalFormat = null): mixed
    {
        if ($value instanceof BackedEnum) {
            return $value->value;
        }
        if ($value instanceof DateTimeInterface) {
            // The schema decides the shape: `format: date` is a DATE, and writing a whole
            // timestamp for it diverged from every other mode. Sub-second precision is kept
            // only when the value carries it, so a plain timestamp is not widened with a
            // `.000000` the client never sent.
            if ($temporalFormat === 'date') {
                return $value->format('Y-m-d');
            }
            if ($temporalFormat === 'time') {
                return $value->format('H:i:sP');
            }

            return $value->format('u') === '000000'
                ? $value->format('c')
                : $value->format('Y-m-d\TH:i:s.uP');
        }
        if (is_object($value) && method_exists($value, 'getData')) {
            // A `type: object` with nothing to write is `{}`, and PHP spells that the same
            // as an empty LIST — so the empty case is cast here, the one place that still
            // knows the value came from an object schema.
            $data = $value->getData();
            return $data === [] ? (object)$data : $data;
        }
        if (is_array($value)) {
            return array_map(
                static fn(mixed $item): mixed => self::openApiWireValue($item, $temporalFormat),
                $value,
            );
        }

        return $value;
    }

    /**
     * Entered once per object by the class-level #[Callback]: `$value` IS this DTO, and the paths are
     * set by the interpreter rather than by a property label.
     */
    private function validateOpenApiConstraints(mixed $value, Callback $rule, ValidationContext $context): Result
    {
        $result = new Result();
        foreach ($this->validateOpenApiNode($this->toOpenApiValidationPayload(), self::OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS, 'payload', 0) as $error) {
            $result->addError(str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
        }

        return $result;
    }

    /**
     * This DTO as an OpenAPI-named payload: what it received, under the names the schema uses.
     * Machinery for the generated validation, not an API to call.
     *
     * @internal
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>
     */
    public function toOpenApiValidationPayload(): array
    {
        $payload = [];
        if ($this->hasProperty('kind')) {
            $payload['kind'] = $this->kind;
        }
        if ($this->hasProperty('side')) {
            $payload['side'] = $this->side;
        }

        return $payload;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
    private function validateOpenApiNode(mixed $value, array $schema, string $path, int $depth): array
    {
        if ($depth >= self::OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH) {
            return [];
        }

        $errors = [];
        $normalizedValue = $value;
        $structuredValue = $this->normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue($value);

        if (array_key_exists('type', $schema)) {
            $typeConstraint = $schema['type'];
            if (is_string($typeConstraint)) {
                if (!$this->matchesOpenApiCallbackType($normalizedValue, $typeConstraint)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s must be of type %s', $path, $typeConstraint);
                }
            }
        }

        if (is_array($structuredValue) && is_array($schema['required'] ?? null)) {
            foreach ($schema['required'] as $requiredProp) {
                if (is_string($requiredProp) && !array_key_exists($requiredProp, $structuredValue)) {
                    $errors[] = sprintf('%s is required', $this->openApiChildPath($path, $requiredProp));
                }
            }
        }

        if (is_array($schema['properties'] ?? null) && is_array($structuredValue)) {
            foreach ($schema['properties'] as $propertyName => $propertySchema) {
                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($propertySchema)) {
                    continue;
                }
                if (!array_key_exists($propertyName, $structuredValue)) {
                    continue;
                }
                if ($this->isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject($structuredValue[$propertyName])) {
                    continue;
                }
                $errors = [
                    ...$errors,
                    ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($structuredValue[$propertyName], $propertySchema, $this->openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        return $errors;
    }

    private function matchesOpenApiCallbackType(mixed $value, mixed $type): bool
    {
        if (!is_string($type) || $type === '') {
            return true;
        }

        return match ($type) {
            'object' => is_array($value) || is_object($value),
            default => true,
        };
    }

    private function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Generated DTOs expose their OpenAPI-named payload themselves.
        if (method_exists($value, 'toOpenApiValidationPayload')) {
            return $value->toOpenApiValidationPayload();
        }
        // Any other object: only its public state is visible, under PHP property names.
        return array_filter(
            get_object_vars($value),
            static fn(mixed $propertyValue): bool => $propertyValue !== null,
        );
    }

    private function isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject(mixed $value): bool
    {
        // Such a DTO validates its own schema through its own #[Assert\Callback], reached by the
        // #[Assert\Valid] cascade — the enclosing DTO must not report the same errors again.
        return is_object($value)
            && method_exists($value, 'validateOpenApiConstraints')
            && method_exists($value, 'toOpenApiValidationPayload');
    }

    private function openApiChildPath(string $path, string $propertyName): string
    {
        // The first level names the field the way the runtime validator's caller does, so both
        // modes report the same subject: `field "tags" must ...`.
        return $path === 'payload'
            ? sprintf('field "%s"', $propertyName)
            : $path . '.' . $propertyName;
    }
}

===== Tag.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use BackedEnum;
use DateTimeInterface;
use ReflectionProperty;
use Yiisoft\Input\Http\AbstractInput;
use Yiisoft\Validator\DataSetInterface;
use Yiisoft\Validator\EmptyCondition\WhenNull;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Helper\ObjectParser;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Result;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Rule\Callback;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Rule\Integer;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Rule\StringValue;
use Yiisoft\Validator\RulesProviderInterface;
use Yiisoft\Validator\ValidationContext;

use function array_filter;
use function array_key_exists;
use function array_map;
use function get_object_vars;
use function is_array;
use function is_object;
use function is_string;
use function method_exists;
use function property_exists;
use function sprintf;
use function str_ends_with;

#[Callback(method: 'validateOpenApiConstraints')]
final class Tag extends AbstractInput implements DataSetInterface, RulesProviderInterface
{

        'id' => 'id',
        'name' => 'name',
    ];

    private const array OPENAPI_WRITE_ONLY = [
    ];

    private const array OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS = [
        'type' => 'object',
        'required' => [
            'id',
            'name',
        ],
        'properties' => [
            'id' => [],
            'name' => [],
        ],
    ];

    private const int OPENAPI_MAX_VALIDATION_DEPTH = 256;

    #[Integer(skipOnEmpty: new WhenNull())]
    public readonly int $id;

    #[StringValue(skipOnEmpty: new WhenNull())]
    public readonly string $name;

    public function getId(): int
    {
        return $this->id;
    }

    public function getName(): string
    {
        return $this->name;
    }

            unset($payload[$writeOnly]);
        }

    }

     * set by the interpreter rather than by a property label.

    {

     *
     * @internal
     *
     * @return array<string, mixed>

    {

        }

     * @return array<int, string>
     */

            return [];

        }

                if (!is_string($propertyName) || !is_array($propertySchema)) {
                    continue;
                }

                    continue;
                }

                    ...$errors,
                    ...$this->validateOpenApiNode($structuredValue[$propertyName], $propertySchema, $this->openApiChildPath($path, $propertyName), $depth + 1),
                ];
            }
        }

        return $errors;
    }

    private function matchesOpenApiCallbackType(mixed $value, mixed $type): bool
    {
        if (!is_string($type) || $type === '') {
            return true;
        }

        return match ($type) {
            'object' => is_array($value) || is_object($value),
            default => true,
        };
    }

    private function normalizeOpenApiStructuralValue(mixed $value): mixed
    {
        if (is_array($value) || !is_object($value)) {
            return $value;
        }

        // Generated DTOs expose their OpenAPI-named payload themselves.
        if (method_exists($value, 'toOpenApiValidationPayload')) {
            return $value->toOpenApiValidationPayload();
        }

        return array_filter(

    private function isGeneratedOpenApiDtoObject(mixed $value): bool

    private function openApiChildPath(string $path, string $propertyName): string

===== Test.php =====
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace GoldenCorpus;

use BackedEnum;
use DateTimeInterface;
use ReflectionProperty;
use Yiisoft\Input\Http\AbstractInput;
use Yiisoft\Validator\DataSetInterface;
use Yiisoft\Validator\EmptyCondition\WhenNull;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Helper\ObjectParser;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Result;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Rule\Callback;
use Yiisoft\Validator\Rule\Integer;
use Yiisoft\Validator\RulesProviderInterface;
use Yiisoft\Validator\ValidationContext;

use function array_filter;
use function array_key_exists;
use function array_map;
use function get_object_vars;
use function is_array;
use function is_object;
use function is_string;
use function method_exists;
use function property_exists;
use function sprintf;
use function str_ends_with;

#[Callback(method: 'validateOpenApiConstraints')]
final class Test extends AbstractInput implements DataSetInterface, RulesProviderInterface
{
    /** OpenAPI name => PHP property name. */
    private const array OPENAPI_PROPERTY_NAMES = [
        'id' => 'id',
    ];

    /** Names not written out: writeOnly by definition, readOnly because a client may not set them. */
    private const array OPENAPI_WRITE_ONLY = [
    ];

    private const array OPENAPI_VALIDATION_CONSTRAINTS = [
        'type' => 'object',
        'required' => [
            'id',
        ],
        'properties' => [
            'id' => [],
        ],
    ];

    {

            $result->addError(str_ends_with($error, '.') ? $error : $error . '.');
        }

        return $result;
    }

     * Machinery for the generated validation, not an API to call.

     */

    {

        if ($this->hasProperty('id')) {
            $payload['id'] = $this->id;
        }

        return $payload;
    }

    /**
     * @param array<string, mixed> $schema
     * @return array<int, string>
     */
